
-- DATABASE: artifacts/authenticated-dom-detection/checkpoint.db

CREATE INDEX comments_video_sample ON comment_samples(video_id,id DESC);
CREATE INDEX creator_history ON creator_profile_observations(creator_id,id DESC);
CREATE INDEX identity_resolver_candidates ON product_identity_pairs(resolver_version,candidate_a_id,candidate_b_id);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX identity_resolver_id ON product_identity_pairs(id) WHERE id IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX observations_capture ON search_observations(capture_id);
CREATE INDEX observations_video_time ON search_observations(video_id,observed_at DESC,id DESC);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX one_active_enrichment ON enrichment_batches((1)) WHERE status='running';
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX one_running_night ON night_discovery_runs((1)) WHERE status='running';
CREATE INDEX trend_target_time ON trend_snapshots(target_type,target_id,observed_at);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX visual_pipeline_input ON visual_pilots(input_hash) WHERE input_hash IS NOT NULL;
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX visual_proposal_idempotency ON visual_product_evidence(idempotency_key) WHERE idempotency_key IS NOT NULL;
CREATE TABLE "bridge_commands" (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                updated_at TEXT NOT NULL, type TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(type IN ('SEARCH','READ_SEARCH_RESULTS','READ_VIDEO_DETAIL','READ_COMMENTS_SAMPLE','READ_CREATOR_PROFILE','DISCOVER_MORE_RESULTS')),
                query TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(status IN ('PENDING','DISPATCHED','SUCCEEDED','FAILED','EXPIRED')),
                extension_id TEXT NOT NULL, detail TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', result_hash TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', options_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',ready_at TEXT);

        score REAL NOT NULL, reason TEXT NOT NULL, evidence_json TEXT NOT NULL,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL, updated_at TEXT NOT NULL, relationship_version TEXT NOT NULL,

                created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                level TEXT NOT NULL,
                event_type TEXT NOT NULL,
                message TEXT NOT NULL
            );
-- ROW COUNT events: 1249
CREATE TABLE missions (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                status TEXT NOT NULL,
                market TEXT NOT NULL,
                mode TEXT NOT NULL,
                max_videos INTEGER NOT NULL,
                read_comments INTEGER NOT NULL,
                capture_evidence INTEGER NOT NULL,
                discover_related INTEGER NOT NULL
            );
-- ROW COUNT missions: 2
CREATE TABLE product_candidate_aliases (
        candidate_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES product_candidates(id),alias TEXT NOT NULL,normalized_alias TEXT NOT NULL,
        source TEXT NOT NULL,confidence REAL NOT NULL,observed_at TEXT NOT NULL,detected_language TEXT,
        UNIQUE(candidate_id,alias,source));
-- ROW COUNT product_candidate_aliases: 8
CREATE TABLE product_candidate_evidence (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,candidate_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES product_candidates(id),evidence_type TEXT NOT NULL,
        source_entity_type TEXT NOT NULL,source_entity_id TEXT NOT NULL,video_id INTEGER REFERENCES videos(id),creator_id INTEGER REFERENCES creators(creator_id),
        commenter TEXT,source_url TEXT NOT NULL,raw_text TEXT NOT NULL,extracted_text TEXT,confidence REAL NOT NULL,
        observed_at TEXT NOT NULL,extractor_version TEXT NOT NULL,dedup_key TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE);
-- ROW COUNT product_candidate_evidence: 65
CREATE TABLE product_candidates (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,platform TEXT NOT NULL,canonical_name TEXT NOT NULL,normalized_name TEXT NOT NULL,
        status TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(status IN ('observed','corroborated','promoted','rejected')),manual_status TEXT CHECK(manual_status IN ('confirmed','rejected')),
        confidence REAL NOT NULL,first_seen_at TEXT NOT NULL,last_seen_at TEXT NOT NULL,primary_reference_url TEXT NOT NULL,
        detection_version TEXT NOT NULL,summary_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}', signature TEXT, broad_concept INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, UNIQUE(platform,normalized_name));
-- ROW COUNT product_candidates: 8
CREATE TABLE product_detection_gaps (
        run_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES product_detection_runs(id),video_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES videos(id),
        reason TEXT NOT NULL,explanation TEXT NOT NULL,source_snapshot_json TEXT NOT NULL,analyzed_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        analysis_version TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(run_id,video_id));
-- ROW COUNT product_detection_gaps: 179
CREATE TABLE product_detection_runs (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,created_at TEXT NOT NULL,detection_version TEXT NOT NULL,input_scope TEXT NOT NULL,
        videos_processed INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,candidates_created INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,candidates_updated INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        evidence_created INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,status TEXT NOT NULL,report_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',config_json TEXT NOT NULL, vocabulary_version TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'product-vocabulary-v1');
-- ROW COUNT product_detection_runs: 22
CREATE TABLE product_video_detection_state (
        video_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES videos(id),detected_at TEXT NOT NULL,vocabulary_version TEXT NOT NULL);
-- ROW COUNT product_video_detection_state: 45
CREATE TABLE product_vocabulary_reviews (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,phrase_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES unresolved_product_phrases(id),
        normalized_phrase TEXT NOT NULL,decision TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(decision IN ('approved','rejected')),
        note TEXT NOT NULL,reviewed_at TEXT NOT NULL);
-- ROW COUNT product_vocabulary_reviews: 0
CREATE TABLE product_vocabulary_state (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(id=1),revision INTEGER NOT NULL);
-- ROW COUNT product_vocabulary_state: 1
CREATE TABLE product_vocabulary_terms (
        normalized_phrase TEXT PRIMARY KEY,raw_phrase TEXT NOT NULL,active INTEGER NOT NULL,updated_at TEXT NOT NULL);

CREATE TABLE search_captures (
            capture_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, search_query TEXT NOT NULL, observed_at TEXT NOT NULL, extractor_version TEXT NOT NULL, new_count INTEGER, updated_count INTEGER);
-- ROW COUNT search_captures: 24
CREATE TABLE search_observations (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, video_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES videos(id),
            capture_id TEXT NOT NULL, search_query TEXT NOT NULL, search_position INTEGER,
            observed_at TEXT NOT NULL, raw_visible_text TEXT, card_snapshot TEXT, extractor_version TEXT NOT NULL,
            extraction_completeness REAL NOT NULL CHECK(extraction_completeness BETWEEN 0 AND 1),
            fields_json TEXT NOT NULL, field_evidence_json TEXT NOT NULL, legacy_id INTEGER UNIQUE, visible_position INTEGER, global_position_estimate INTEGER, scroll_iteration INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
            UNIQUE(capture_id,video_id));
-- ROW COUNT search_observations: 167

                key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                value_json TEXT NOT NULL,
                updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
            );

CREATE TABLE unresolved_phrase_observations (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,phrase_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES unresolved_product_phrases(id),
        source_entity_type TEXT NOT NULL,source_entity_id TEXT NOT NULL,raw_text TEXT NOT NULL,source_url TEXT NOT NULL,
        observed_at TEXT NOT NULL,method TEXT NOT NULL,
        UNIQUE(phrase_id,source_entity_type,source_entity_id,raw_text));
-- ROW COUNT unresolved_phrase_observations: 4
CREATE TABLE unresolved_product_phrases (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,video_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES videos(id),raw_phrase TEXT NOT NULL,
        normalized_phrase TEXT NOT NULL,signature TEXT NOT NULL,status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending'
        CHECK(status IN ('pending','approved','rejected')),first_seen_at TEXT NOT NULL,last_seen_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        discovery_version TEXT NOT NULL,UNIQUE(video_id,normalized_phrase));
-- ROW COUNT unresolved_product_phrases: 2

        video_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES videos(id),creator_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES creators(creator_id),
        linked_at TEXT NOT NULL,source TEXT NOT NULL);

CREATE TABLE video_detail_observations (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, video_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES videos(id),
            command_id INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE, observed_at TEXT NOT NULL, extractor_version TEXT NOT NULL,
            raw_visible_text TEXT, card_snapshot TEXT, fields_json TEXT NOT NULL, field_evidence_json TEXT NOT NULL);
-- ROW COUNT video_detail_observations: 21
CREATE TABLE video_enrichment_queue (
        video_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES videos(id),needs_detail INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
        needs_comments INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,needs_creator INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
        needs_product_detection INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,enrichment_priority INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,updated_at TEXT NOT NULL);
-- ROW COUNT video_enrichment_queue: 28
CREATE TABLE video_observations (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                source TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'tiktok' CHECK(source='tiktok'),
                search_query TEXT NOT NULL,
                video_url TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                creator_username TEXT, creator_display_name TEXT, caption TEXT,
                views TEXT, likes TEXT, comments_count TEXT, shares TEXT,
                collected_at TEXT NOT NULL, first_seen_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                last_seen_at TEXT NOT NULL, raw_visible_text TEXT,
                extraction_version TEXT NOT NULL
            );
-- ROW COUNT video_observations: 8
CREATE TABLE videos (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, source TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(source='tiktok'),
            video_id TEXT, canonical_url TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE, creator_username TEXT, creator_display_name TEXT,
            caption TEXT, thumbnail_url TEXT, first_seen_at TEXT NOT NULL, last_seen_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            field_evidence_json TEXT NOT NULL, first_search_query TEXT, latest_search_query TEXT, UNIQUE(source,video_id));
-- ROW COUNT videos: 45
